import os, subprocess, time, hashlib, datetime, re
from pathlib import Path
root=Path.cwd(); out=root/'.cache/sessions-phase0'
env=dict(os.environ,CGO_ENABLED='0',GOTOOLCHAIN='local',GOAMD64='v1',GOMAXPROCS='16')
start=time.monotonic()
def sha(path): return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
print('UTC',datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),flush=True)
print('qualification_base_commit',subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),flush=True)
print('Go1.26.8 CGO0 GOAMD64v1 PGOoff defaultGOGC/GOMEMLIMIT; noaffinity; benchmark-isolated tools and agents idle',flush=True)
for name in ['dd-baseline.test','dd-r10.test','stream-baseline-r10.test','stream-r10.test']:
 print(name,sha(out/name),flush=True)
for name in ['rt/instance.go','rt/batch.go','internal/gen/gogen/gogen.go','internal/gen/gogen/batch.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/checked_bench_test.go','testdata/streaming/cocoon.lock.json','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm']:
 print(name,sha(root/name),flush=True)
baseline=Path((out/'phase0-snapshot').read_text().strip())
print('baseline_snapshot',baseline,flush=True)
for name in ['rt/instance.go','internal/gen/gogen/gogen.go','testdata/streaming/cocoon.toml','testdata/streaming/cocoon.lock.json','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go']:
 print('baseline/'+name,sha(baseline/name),flush=True)
assert sha(baseline/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm')==sha(root/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm')
assert sha(baseline/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go')==sha(root/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go')
for key in ['GOGC','GOMEMLIMIT','GODEBUG']:
 print(key,env.get(key,'<unset>'),flush=True)
print('affinity',sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)),flush=True)
def sample(binary,pattern,duration,name):
 args=[str(out/binary),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+pattern,'-test.benchtime='+duration,'-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
 with (out/name).open('w') as log:
  log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# initialGOMAXPROCS=16; explicit P leaf sets actual runtime procs\n');log.flush()
  subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
 measured_rows=0
 for row in (out/name).read_text().splitlines():
  fields=row.split()
  if not fields or not fields[0].startswith('BenchmarkStream') or 'ns/op' not in fields: continue
  measured_rows+=1
  match=re.search(r'/P([0-9]+)/',fields[0])
  expected=int(match[1]) if match else 16
  assert 'actual-procs' in fields, 'missing actual-procs: '+row
  actual=float(fields[fields.index('actual-procs')-1])
  assert actual==expected, f'wrong actual-procs {actual}, expected {expected}: {row}'
 assert not binary.startswith('stream') or measured_rows>0, 'no streaming rows: '+name
 print(name+' complete elapsed '+str(round(time.monotonic()-start,1))+'s',flush=True)
dd='^Benchmark(ObfuscateSQL|SketchAdd|SketchAddMany1k|ObfuscateTraces1k|SketchAddVaried|ObfuscateSQLVaried|SketchAddNegativeBins)$'

=== .cache/sessions-phase0/confirm-r10-dd.py ===
from pathlib import Path
import os, subprocess, time, datetime, hashlib
out=Path('.cache/sessions-phase0').resolve(); env=dict(os.environ,CGO_ENABLED='0',GOTOOLCHAIN='local',GOAMD64='v1',GOMAXPROCS='16')
pattern='^Benchmark(ObfuscateSQL|SketchAdd|SketchAddMany1k|ObfuscateTraces1k|SketchAddVaried|ObfuscateSQLVaried|SketchAddNegativeBins)$'
start=time.monotonic()
print('Independent confirmation of small negative scalar holdout; unchanged frozen binaries; all seven metrics retained; same isolation/flags,500ms,7alternating pairs',flush=True)
for label in ['baseline','r10']: print(label,hashlib.sha256((out/f'dd-{label}.test').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),flush=True)
for rep in range(1,8):
    for label in (['r10','baseline'] if rep%2 else ['baseline','r10']):
        args=[str(out/f'dd-{label}.test'),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+pattern,'-test.benchtime=500ms','-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
        with (out/f'confirm-r10-dd-{label}-{rep}.txt').open('w') as log:
            log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# GOMAXPROCS16 CGO0 Go1.26.8 GOAMD64v1 PGOoff; isolated unchanged binaries\n');log.flush()
            subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        print(label,rep,'elapsed',round(time.monotonic()-start,1),flush=True)
print('CONFIRMATION COMPLETE',flush=True)

 print(key,env.get(key,'<unset>'),flush=True)
print('affinity',sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)),flush=True)
def sample(binary,pattern,duration,name):
 args=[str(out/binary),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+pattern,'-test.benchtime='+duration,'-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
 with (out/name).open('w') as log:
  log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# initialGOMAXPROCS=16; explicit P leaf sets actual runtime procs\n');log.flush()
  subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
 measured_rows=0
 for row in (out/name).read_text().splitlines():
  fields=row.split()
  if not fields or not fields[0].startswith('BenchmarkStream') or 'ns/op' not in fields: continue
  measured_rows+=1
  match=re.search(r'/P([0-9]+)/',fields[0])
  expected=int(match[1]) if match else 16
  assert 'actual-procs' in fields, 'missing actual-procs: '+row
  actual=float(fields[fields.index('actual-procs')-1])
  assert actual==expected, f'wrong actual-procs {actual}, expected {expected}: {row}'
 assert not binary.startswith('stream') or measured_rows>0, 'no streaming rows: '+name
 print(name+' complete elapsed '+str(round(time.monotonic()-start,1))+'s',flush=True)
dd='^Benchmark(ObfuscateSQL|SketchAdd|SketchAddMany1k|ObfuscateTraces1k|SketchAddVaried|ObfuscateSQLVaried|SketchAddNegativeBins)$'
stand='^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P(1|4|16)$/^Chunk(64|4096|16384)$/^Retain32$'
checked='^BenchmarkStreamChecked$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P(1|4|16)$/^Chunk(64|4096|16384)$/^N32$/^(StandaloneHomesP|BatchHomes1|BatchHomesP)$'
length_stand='^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^Fixed$/^P16$/^Chunk(64|4096)$/^Retain(16|64|256)$'
length_checked='^BenchmarkStreamChecked$/^Fixed$/^P16$/^Chunk(64|4096)$/^N(16|64|256)$/^(StandaloneHomesP|BatchHomes1|BatchHomesP)$'
for rep in range(1,8):
 for label in (['r10','baseline'] if rep%2 else ['baseline','r10']): sample('stream-baseline-r10.test' if label=='baseline' else 'stream-r10.test',stand,'250ms',f'confirm-r10-stream-{label}-{rep}.txt')
print('INDEPENDENT STREAM CONFIRMATION COMPLETE elapsed '+str(round(time.monotonic()-start,1))+'s',flush=True)
